```python
from logging.config import fileConfig

from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config
from sqlalchemy import pool

from alembic import context

from dotenv import load_dotenv
import os

from app.database import Base
from app.models.user import User
from app.models.post import Post


# this is the Alembic Config object, which provides
# access to the values within the .ini file in use.
config = context.config

load_dotenv()

database_url = os.getenv("DATABASE_URL")

config.set_main_option(
    "sqlalchemy.url",
    database_url
)


# Interpret the config file for Python logging.
# This line sets up loggers basically.
if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

# add your model's MetaData object here
# for 'autogenerate' support
# from myapp import mymodel
# target_metadata = mymodel.Base.metadata
target_metadata = Base.metadata

# other values from the config, defined by the needs of env.py,
# can be acquired:
# my_important_option = config.get_main_option("my_important_option")
# ... etc.


def run_migrations_offline() -> None:
    """Run migrations in 'offline' mode.

    This configures the context with just a URL
    and not an Engine, though an Engine is acceptable
    here as well.  By skipping the Engine creation
    we don't even need a DBAPI to be available.

    Calls to context.execute() here emit the given string to the
    script output.

    """
    url = config.get_main_option("sqlalchemy.url")
    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
    )

    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online() -> None:
    """Run migrations in 'online' mode.

    In this scenario we need to create an Engine
    and associate a connection with the context.

    """
    connectable = engine_from_config(
        config.get_section(config.config_ini_section, {}),
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )

    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(
            connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata
        )

        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()
```

```python
class ApplicationError(Exception):
    """Base exception for application/business errors."""

    def __init__(self, message: str):
        self.message = message
        super().__init__(message)


class ResourceNotFoundError(ApplicationError):
    """Requested resource does not exist."""


class PermissionDeniedError(ApplicationError):
    """Authenticated user is not allowed to perform the action."""


class ConflictError(ApplicationError):
    """Operation conflicts with the current resource state."""
```

```python
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from jose import jwt
from pwdlib import PasswordHash

from app.core.config import settings


password_hash = [REDACTED]


def hash_password(password: [REDACTED] -> str:
    return password_hash.hash(password)


def verify_password(
    plain_password: [REDACTED],
    hashed_password: [REDACTED],
) -> bool:
    return password_hash.verify(
        plain_password,
        hashed_password,
    )


def create_access_token(data: dict) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()

    expire = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(
        minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES
    )

    to_encode.update(
        {
            "exp": expire,
        }
    )

    encoded_jwt = jwt.encode(
        to_encode,
        settings.SECRET_KEY,
        algorithm=settings.ALGORITHM,
    )

    return encoded_jwt
```

## File: `app/dependencies.py`

**Size:** 2,348 bytes | **Lines included:** 99 | **Secret values redacted:** 2

```python
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.config import settings
from app.database import get_db
from app.models import User, user
from app.services.auth_service import AuthService
from app.services.post_service import PostService
from app.services.user_service import UserService


oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(
    tokenUrl="[REDACTED]",
)


def get_user_service(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> UserService:
    return UserService(db)


def get_post_service(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> PostService:
    return PostService(db)


def get_auth_service(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
) -> AuthService:
    return AuthService(db)


def get_current_user(
    token: [REDACTED] = Depends(oauth2_scheme),
    service: AuthService = Depends(get_auth_service),
) -> User:
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Could not validate credentials",
        headers={
            "WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer",
        },
    )

    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(
            token,
            settings.SECRET_KEY,
            algorithms=[settings.ALGORITHM],
        )

        subject = payload.get("sub")

        if not isinstance(subject, str) or not subject:
            raise credentials_exception

    except JWTError as exc:
        raise credentials_exception from exc

    # New token format:
    # sub = string representation of user.id
    if subject.isdigit():
        user = service.get_user_by_id(
            int(subject)
        )

    # Backward compatibility:
    # old tokens used email as the JWT subject.
    else:
        user = service.get_user_by_email(
            subject
        )

    if user is None:
        raise credentials_exception

    if not user.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Inactive user",
        )

    return user


def get_admin_user(
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
) -> User:
    if current_user.role != "admin":
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail="Admin access required",
        )

    return current_user
```

```python
PAGINATION_HEADERS_OPENAPI = {
    "X-Total-Count": {
        "description": (
            "Total number of records matching "
            "the current filters."
        ),
        "schema": {
            "type": "integer",
        },
    },
    "X-Limit": {
        "description": (
            "Maximum number of records "
            "requested for this page."
        ),
        "schema": {
            "type": "integer",
        },
    },
    "X-Offset": {
        "description": (
            "Number of records skipped "
            "before this page."
        ),
        "schema": {
            "type": "integer",
        },
    },
    "X-Has-More": {
        "description": (
            "Whether more matching records "
            "exist after this page."
        ),
        "schema": {
            "type": "boolean",
        },
    },
}


def build_pagination_headers(
    *,
    total: int,
    limit: int,
    offset: int,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    has_more = (
        offset + limit
        < total
    )

    return {
        "X-Total-Count": str(total),
        "X-Limit": str(limit),
        "X-Offset": str(offset),
        "X-Has-More": (
            "true"
            if has_more
            else "false"
        ),
    }
```

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Create a prioritized, size-limited and secret-redacted project_context.md.

Usage:
    python build_project_context.py
    python build_project_context.py --root . --output project_context.md
    python build_project_context.py --max-total-chars 450000

Standard library only. Put this script in the project root for the simplest use.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import fnmatch
import os
import re
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

VERSION = "2.0"

EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    ".git", "__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".mypy_cache", ".ruff_cache",
    ".hypothesis", ".tox", ".nox", ".venv", "venv", "env", "node_modules",
    ".next", ".nuxt", ".turbo", ".vercel", ".netlify", "dist", "build",
    "coverage", "htmlcov", ".cache", ".idea", ".vscode", ".terraform",
    ".serverless", "site-packages", "vendor", "target", "logs", ".ssh",
    ".aws", ".azure", ".kube", "certificates", "certs",
}

# .sql is essential for PostgreSQL projects and is deliberately included.
ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".py", ".pyi", ".sql", ".md", ".mdx", ".rst", ".txt", ".ini",
    ".cfg", ".toml", ".yaml", ".yml", ".json", ".sh", ".bash", ".ps1",
    ".js", ".jsx", ".mjs", ".cjs", ".ts", ".tsx", ".html", ".xml", ".css",
    ".dockerfile",
}
ALLOWED_FILENAMES = {
    "Dockerfile", "LICENSE", "Makefile", "Procfile", "Pipfile", "Justfile",
    "Taskfile", "alembic.ini", ".gitignore", ".dockerignore", ".editorconfig",
}
EXCLUDED_FILENAMES = {
    "project_context.md", "package-lock.json", "npm-shrinkwrap.json", "yarn.lock",
    "pnpm-lock.yaml", "poetry.lock", "uv.lock", "Pipfile.lock", ".DS_Store",
    "Thumbs.db",
}
EXCLUDED_PATTERNS = {
    "*.lock", "*.log", "*.pyc", "*.pyo", "*.pyd", "*.so", "*.dll", "*.exe",
    "*.bin", "*.db", "*.sqlite", "*.sqlite3", "*.parquet", "*.csv", "*.tsv",
    "*.xlsx", "*.xls", "*.pdf", "*.zip", "*.7z", "*.tar", "*.gz", "*.png",
    "*.jpg", "*.jpeg", "*.gif", "*.webp", "*.ico", "*.mp3", "*.mp4", "*.mov",
    "*.woff", "*.woff2", "*.ttf", "*.otf", "*.map", "*.min.js", "*.min.css",
    "*.generated.*",
}
SENSITIVE_FILENAME_PATTERNS = {
    ".env*", "*credentials*", "*service-account*", "*private-key*", "id_rsa*",
    "id_ed25519*", "*.pem", "*.key", "*.p12", "*.pfx", "*.crt", "*.cer", "*.der",
}

SECRET_NAME = [REDACTED]
    r"[\w.-]*(?:PASSWORD|PASSWD|SECRET|TOKEN|API[_-]?KEY|PRIVATE[_-]?KEY|"
    r"DATABASE_URL|DB_URL|DSN|CONNECTION_STRING|CLIENT_SECRET|ACCESS_KEY)[\w.-]*"
)
QUOTED_SECRET_RE = [REDACTED]
    rf"(?i)(?P<prefix>[\"']?{SECRET_NAME}[\"']?\s*[:=]\s*)"
    r"(?P<quote>[\"'])(?P<value>.*?)(?P=quote)"
)
UNQUOTED_SECRET_RE = [REDACTED]
    rf"(?i)(?P<prefix>\b{SECRET_NAME}\b\s*[:=]\s*)"
    r"(?P<value>(?![\"'])([^\s,;#]+))"
)
PEM_RE = re.compile(
    r"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |DSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----.*?"
    r"-----END (?:RSA |EC |OPENSSH |DSA )?PRIVATE KEY-----", re.DOTALL
)
BEARER_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)\b(Bearer\s+)[A-Za-z0-9._~+/=-]+")
URL_PASSWORD_RE = [REDACTED]
    r"(?i)(https?://[^:/\s]+:|postgres(?:ql)?://[^:/\s]+:)([^@\s/]+)(@)"
)
JWT_RE = re.compile(r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{8,}\b")
TOKEN_PATTERNS = [REDACTED]
    re.compile(r"\bgh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bgithub_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bxox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bAKIA[0-9A-Z]{16}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bAIza[0-9A-Za-z_-]{30,}\b"),
    re.compile(r"\bsk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}\b"),
]
LANGUAGES = {
    ".py": "python", ".pyi": "python", ".sql": "sql", ".md": "markdown",
    ".mdx": "mdx", ".rst": "rst", ".txt": "text", ".ini": "ini",
    ".cfg": "ini", ".toml": "toml", ".yaml": "yaml", ".yml": "yaml",
    ".json": "json", ".sh": "bash", ".bash": "bash", ".ps1": "powershell",
    ".js": "javascript", ".jsx": "jsx", ".mjs": "javascript", ".cjs": "javascript",
    ".ts": "typescript", ".tsx": "tsx", ".html": "html", ".xml": "xml",
    ".css": "css", ".dockerfile": "dockerfile",
}


def parse_args() -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Build a safe project context Markdown file.")
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=Path(__file__).resolve().parent,
                        help="Project root; defaults to the folder containing this script.")
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path, default=None,
                        help="Output path; defaults to <root>/project_context.md.")
    parser.add_argument("--max-file-mb", type=float, default=0.30,
                        help="Skip individual files larger than this size; default 0.30 MB.")
    parser.add_argument("--max-file-chars", type=int, default=60000,
                        help="Maximum characters included from one file; default 60000.")
    parser.add_argument("--max-total-chars", type=int, default=350000,
                        help="Maximum combined source-content characters; default 350000.")
    return parser.parse_args()


def is_inside(path: Path, parent: Path) -> bool:
    try:
        path.relative_to(parent)
        return True
    except ValueError:
        return False


def should_include(path: Path, root: Path, output: Path) -> bool:
    if path.is_symlink():
        return False
    try:
        resolved = path.resolve()
        if resolved == output or not is_inside(resolved, root):
            return False
    except OSError:
        return False

    relative_parts = path.relative_to(root).parts
    if any(part.lower() in EXCLUDED_DIRS for part in relative_parts[:-1]):
        return False

    name = path.name
    lower_name = name.lower()
    suffix = path.suffix.lower()

    # Exclude every .env variant, not just a file literally named .env.
    if lower_name.startswith(".env"):
        return False
    if lower_name in {item.lower() for item in EXCLUDED_FILENAMES}:
        return False
    if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(lower_name, p) for p in SENSITIVE_FILENAME_PATTERNS):
        return False
    if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(lower_name, p) for p in EXCLUDED_PATTERNS):
        return False
    if suffix in ALLOWED_EXTENSIONS:
        return True
    if name in ALLOWED_FILENAMES or lower_name in {x.lower() for x in ALLOWED_FILENAMES}:
        return True
    return lower_name.startswith("requirements") and suffix == ".txt"


def priority_for(relative: str) -> int:
    """Lower score means earlier inclusion when the context budget is limited."""
    path = relative.lower()
    parts = set(path.split("/"))
    filename = path.rsplit("/", 1)[-1]

    if filename in {
        "readme.md", "pyproject.toml", "requirements.txt", "docker-compose.yml",
        "docker-compose.yaml", "compose.yml", "compose.yaml", "alembic.ini",
    }:
        return 0
    if filename in {"main.py", "database.py", "db.py", "config.py", "settings.py"}:
        return 1
    if parts.intersection({
        "models", "schemas", "routers", "routes", "repositories", "services",
        "crud", "dependencies", "migrations", "versions", "sql", "analytics",
    }):
        return 1
    if "tests" in parts:
        return 2
    if "mentor-web" in parts or ".github" in parts:
        return 3
    if "docs" in parts:
        return 4
    return 3


def collect_files(root: Path, output: Path) -> List[Dict[str, object]]:
    records: List[Dict[str, object]] = []
    for current, directories, filenames in os.walk(root, topdown=True, followlinks=False):
        current_path = Path(current)
        directories[:] = sorted(
            d for d in directories
            if d.lower() not in EXCLUDED_DIRS and not (current_path / d).is_symlink()
        )
        for filename in sorted(filenames):
            path = current_path / filename
            if not should_include(path, root, output):
                continue
            try:
                size = path.stat().st_size
            except OSError:
                continue
            relative = path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
            records.append({
                "path": path,
                "relative": relative,
                "size": size,
                "priority": priority_for(relative),
            })
    records.sort(key=lambda x: (int(x["priority"]), str(x["relative"]).lower()))
    return records


def redact_secrets(content: str) -> Tuple[str, int]:
    """Best-effort redaction; no automatic scanner can guarantee secret detection."""
    count_total = 0

    def quoted_replacement(match: re.Match) -> str:
        return match.group("prefix") + match.group("quote") + "[REDACTED]" + match.group("quote")

    content, count = QUOTED_SECRET_RE.subn(quoted_replacement, content)
    count_total += count

    safe_rhs = (
        "os.getenv", "os.environ", "environ[", "settings.", "config.", "secretstr",
        "field(", "${", "$", "none", "null", "true", "false", "required",
    )

    def unquoted_replacement(match: re.Match) -> str:
        nonlocal count_total
        value = match.group("value")
        if value.lower().startswith(safe_rhs):
            return match.group(0)
        count_total += 1
        return match.group("prefix") + "[REDACTED]"

    content = UNQUOTED_SECRET_RE.sub(unquoted_replacement, content)
    for pattern, replacement in (
        (PEM_RE, "[REDACTED PRIVATE KEY BLOCK]"),
        (BEARER_RE, r"\1[REDACTED]"),
        (URL_PASSWORD_RE, r"\1[REDACTED]\3"),
        (JWT_RE, "[REDACTED JWT]"),
    ):
        content, count = pattern.subn(replacement, content)
        count_total += count
    for pattern in TOKEN_PATTERNS:
        [REDACTED], count = pattern.subn("[REDACTED TOKEN]", content)
        count_total += count
    return content, count_total


def read_text_file(path: Path) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
    try:
        raw = path.read_bytes()
    except OSError as error:
        return None, f"read error: {error}"
    if b"\x00" in raw:
        return None, "binary file detected"
    try:
        return raw.decode("utf-8-sig"), None
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return None, "not valid UTF-8 text"


def truncate_text(content: str, limit: int) -> Tuple[str, bool]:
    if len(content) <= limit:
        return content, False
    marker = "\n\n[... CONTENT TRUNCATED BY PROJECT CONTEXT BUILDER ...]\n\n"
    if limit <= len(marker) + 20:
        return content[:limit], True
    available = limit - len(marker)
    head_size = int(available * 0.75)
    tail_size = available - head_size
    head = content[:head_size]
    tail = content[-tail_size:]
    if "\n" in head:
        head = head.rsplit("\n", 1)[0]
    if "\n" in tail:
        tail = tail.split("\n", 1)[-1]
    return head + marker + tail, True


def make_fence(content: str) -> str:
    runs = re.findall(r"`+", content)
    longest = max((len(run) for run in runs), default=0)
    return "`" * max(3, longest + 1)


def language_for(path: Path) -> str:
    if path.name.lower() == "dockerfile":
        return "dockerfile"
    if path.name.lower() in {".gitignore", ".dockerignore"}:
        return "gitignore"
    if path.name.lower() == "license":
        return "text"
    return LANGUAGES.get(path.suffix.lower(), "")


def render_file_block(record: Dict[str, object], content: str,
                      redactions: int, truncated: bool) -> str:
    path = record["path"]
    assert isinstance(path, Path)
    relative = str(record["relative"])
    fence = make_fence(content)
    lines = content.count("\n") + (1 if content else 0)
    metadata = (
        f"**Size:** {int(record['size']):,} bytes | **Lines included:** {lines:,} | "
        f"**Secret values redacted:** {redactions}"
    )
    if truncated:
        metadata += " | **Note:** content truncated to respect context limits"
    ending = "" if content.endswith("\n") else "\n"
    return (
        f"\n\n---\n\n## File: `{relative}`\n\n{metadata}\n\n"
        f"{fence}{language_for(path)}\n{content}{ending}{fence}\n"
    )


def git_info(root: Path) -> str:
    try:
        branch = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(root), "branch", "--show-current"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False,
        )
        commit = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(root), "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3, check=False,
        )
        b = branch.stdout.strip() if branch.returncode == 0 else ""
        c = commit.stdout.strip() if commit.returncode == 0 else ""
        if not b and not c:
            return "Git metadata: unavailable"
        return f"Git branch: `{b or 'unknown'}`  \nGit commit: `{c or 'unknown'}`"
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        return "Git metadata: unavailable"


def build_report(root: Path, records: List[Dict[str, object]], max_file_bytes: int,
                 max_file_chars: int, max_total_chars: int) -> Tuple[str, Dict[str, int]]:
    blocks: List[str] = []
    omitted: List[Tuple[str, str]] = []
    included = redaction_total = truncated_total = content_chars = included_bytes = 0

    for record in records:
        relative = str(record["relative"])
        size = int(record["size"])
        if size > max_file_bytes:
            omitted.append((relative, f"file too large ({size:,} bytes)"))
            continue
        content, error = read_text_file(record["path"])  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        if error:
            omitted.append((relative, error))
            continue
        assert content is not None
        content, redactions = redact_secrets(content)
        content, truncated = truncate_text(content, max_file_chars)
        block = render_file_block(record, content, redactions, truncated)
        if content_chars + len(block) > max_total_chars:
            omitted.append((relative, "total context character budget reached"))
            continue
        blocks.append(block)
        content_chars += len(block)
        included += 1
        included_bytes += size
        redaction_total += redactions
        truncated_total += int(truncated)

    timestamp = datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    header = f"""# PROJECT CONTEXT

> Generated by `build_project_context.py` v{VERSION}. This is a repository snapshot, not a live connection.

## Snapshot Metadata

- **Project:** `{root.name}`
- **Generated at:** `{timestamp}`
- **Candidate files discovered:** {len(records)}
- **Files included:** {included}
- **Files omitted:** {len(omitted)}
- **Original size of included files:** {included_bytes:,} bytes
- **Source content included:** {content_chars:,} characters
- **Secret-like values redacted:** {redaction_total}
- **Files truncated:** {truncated_total}
- **Maximum source-content budget:** {max_total_chars:,} characters

{git_info(root)}

## Instructions for the AI Assistant

1. Treat the paths and source contents below as the repository snapshot at generation time.
2. Do not assume an omitted file, endpoint, table, column, dependency, or feature exists.
3. Follow the project's existing architecture, naming conventions, and dependency choices.
4. For database questions, inspect SQL files, ORM models, schemas, and migrations together when available.
5. Do not invent database columns, API routes, environment variables, or test results.
6. Distinguish verified source-code facts from assumptions and recommendations.
7. Never ask the user to publish passwords, API keys, tokens, private keys, or `.env` contents.
8. This snapshot can become outdated; request a fresh one when the current repository state matters.
9. Do not edit this generated context file as if it were source code; edit the original project files.

## Project File Inventory

Files are ordered by relevance: configuration and entry points, core application/database code, tests, frontend, then documentation.

"""
    inventory = "\n".join(
        f"- `{r['relative']}` ({int(r['size']):,} bytes; priority {r['priority']})"
        for r in records
    ) or "- No eligible text files were found."
    omitted_text = ""
    if omitted:
        omitted_text = "\n\n## Omitted Files and Reasons\n\n" + "\n".join(
            f"- `{name}` — {reason}" for name, reason in omitted
        )
    report = (
        header + inventory + omitted_text + "\n\n## Source File Contents\n"
        + "".join(blocks) + "\n\n---\n\n## End of Project Context\n"
    )
    return report, {
        "discovered": len(records), "included": included, "omitted": len(omitted),
        "redactions": redaction_total, "truncated": truncated_total,
        "content_chars": content_chars, "included_bytes": included_bytes,
    }


def main() -> None:
    args = parse_args()
    root = args.root.expanduser().resolve()
    if not root.exists() or not root.is_dir():
        raise SystemExit(f"ERROR: Project root is not a directory: {root}")

    if args.output is None:
        output = root / "project_context.md"
    else:
        output = args.output.expanduser()
        if not output.is_absolute():
            output = root / output
        output = output.resolve()
    output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    if args.max_file_mb <= 0 or args.max_file_chars <= 0 or args.max_total_chars <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("ERROR: All size limits must be greater than zero.")

    print("Scanning project files...")
    records = collect_files(root, output)
    print("Prioritizing source files and redacting common secret patterns...")
    report, stats = build_report(
        root, records, int(args.max_file_mb * 1024 * 1024),
        args.max_file_chars, args.max_total_chars,
    )
    output.write_text(report, encoding="utf-8")

    print("\nDone!")
    print(f"Project root:       {root}")
    print(f"Output:             {output}")
    print(f"Files discovered:   {stats['discovered']}")
    print(f"Files included:     {stats['included']}")
    print(f"Files omitted:      {stats['omitted']}")
    print(f"Secret redactions:  {stats['redactions']}")
    print(f"Files truncated:    {stats['truncated']}")
    print(f"Context characters: {len(report):,}")
    print("\nReview the generated Markdown before uploading it.")
    print("Secret redaction is best-effort; never store real credentials in source files.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

```markdown
<!-- BEGIN:nextjs-agent-rules -->

## This is NOT the Next.js you know

This version has breaking changes — APIs, conventions, and file structure may all differ from your training data. Read the relevant guide in `node_modules/next/dist/docs/` (resolved from this file's directory; in monorepos the `next` package may not be visible from the repo root) before writing any code. Heed deprecation notices.

This block is written and re-added by `next dev` — verify at `node_modules/next/dist/server/lib/generate-agent-files.js`. Removing it from a diff only re-creates the uncommitted change; committing it with your work keeps the tree clean.

<!-- END:nextjs-agent-rules -->
```

```javascript
import fs from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
const webRoot = process.cwd();
const repoRoot = path.resolve(webRoot, "..");

const diaryDir = path.join(repoRoot, "docs", "diary");
const analyticsDir = path.join(repoRoot, "sql", "analytics");

const outputFile = path.join(
  webRoot,
  "src",
  "data",
  "progress.generated.json"
);

const analyticsKpiFile = path.join(
  webRoot,
  "src",
  "data",
  "analytics-kpi.snapshot.json"
);

function readText(filePath) {
  return fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
}

function cleanMarkdown(text) {
  return text
    .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, "$1")
    .replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g, "$1")
    .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]+\)/g, "$1")
    .trim();
}

function findLastNumber(text, patterns, fallback) {
  const matches = [];

  for (const pattern of patterns) {
    const globalPattern = new RegExp(
      pattern.source,
      pattern.flags.includes("g")
        ? pattern.flags
        : `${pattern.flags}g`
    );

    for (const match of text.matchAll(globalPattern)) {
      matches.push({
        value: Number(match[1].replaceAll(",", "")),
        index: match.index ?? -1,
      });
    }
  }

  if (matches.length === 0) {
    return fallback;
  }

  matches.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);

  return matches.at(-1).value;
}

function extractBullets(markdown, limit = 8) {
  return markdown
    .split("\n")
    .map((line) => line.trim())
    .filter((line) => /^[-*]\s+/.test(line))
    .map((line) => cleanMarkdown(line.replace(/^[-*]\s+/, "")))
    .filter((line) => line.length > 8 && line.length < 150)
    .slice(0, limit);
}

function buildHighlights(markdown, stats) {
  const bullets = extractBullets(markdown, 50);

  const usefulBullets = bullets.filter((line) => {
    const normalized = line.toLowerCase();

    const usefulKeyword =
      normalized.includes("complete") ||
      normalized.includes("completed") ||
      normalized.includes("implement") ||
      normalized.includes("verified") ||
      normalized.includes("migration") ||
      normalized.includes("test") ||
      normalized.includes("analytics") ||
      normalized.includes("dataset") ||
      normalized.includes("sql") ||
      normalized.includes("lifecycle");

    const looksLikeFieldName =
      line.endsWith(";") ||
      /^[a-z0-9_]+;?$/i.test(line);

    return (
      usefulKeyword &&
      !looksLikeFieldName &&
      line.length >= 20 &&
      line.length <= 140
    );
  });

  const unique = [...new Set(usefulBullets)];

  if (unique.length >= 5) {
    return unique.slice(0, 8);
  }

  return [
    "Completed User and Post lifecycle support",
    "Verified authentication and inactive-user lifecycle",
    `Reached ${stats.tests} passing automated tests`,
    `Prepared ${stats.analyticsUsers.toLocaleString()} synthetic analytics users`,
    `Analyzed ${stats.analyticsPosts.toLocaleString()} synthetic posts`,
    "Completed exploratory SQL and KPI analysis",
    "Practiced business-question analysis",
    "Practiced SQL window functions",
  ];
}

function extractWeek(text) {
  const match = text.match(/\bW\d{1,2}\b/i);
  return match ? match[0].toUpperCase() : "Latest";
}

function formatDate(dateString) {
  const date = new Date(`${dateString}T00:00:00Z`);

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en", {
    day: "2-digit",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
    timeZone: "UTC",
  }).format(date);
}

function findLatestDiary() {
  const files = fs
    .readdirSync(diaryDir)
    .filter((name) => name.endsWith(".md"))
    .filter((name) => name !== "README.md");

  const rangedProgressFiles = files
    .filter((name) =>
      /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-to-\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-current-progress\.md$/.test(
        name
      )
    )
    .sort();

  const summaryFiles = files
    .filter((name) =>
      /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-project-summary\.md$/.test(name)
    )
    .sort();

  const latestProgress =
    rangedProgressFiles.at(-1) ?? null;

  const latestSummary =
    summaryFiles.at(-1) ?? null;

  return {
    latestProgress,
    latestSummary,
  };
}

function buildAnalyticsRoadmap() {
  const files = fs
    .readdirSync(analyticsDir)
    .filter((name) => name.endsWith(".sql"));

  const stages = [
    {
      file: "01_seed_analytics.sql",
      title: "Dataset Preparation",
    },
    {
      file: "02_exploration.sql",
      title: "Exploratory SQL",
    },
    {
      file: "03_kpi_analysis.sql",
      title: "KPI Analysis",
    },
    {
      file: "04_business_questions.sql",
      title: "Business Questions",
    },
    {
      file: "05_window_functions.sql",
      title: "Window Functions",
    },
  ];

  return stages.map((stage) => ({
    ...stage,
    completed: files.includes(stage.file),
  }));
}

const monthlyPosts = JSON.parse(
  readText(
    path.join(
      webRoot,
      "src",
      "data",
      "monthly-posts.snapshot.json"
    )
  )
);

if (
  !Array.isArray(monthlyPosts) ||
  monthlyPosts.some(
    (row) =>
      typeof row.month !== "string" ||
      !/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(row.month) ||
      !Number.isInteger(row.posts) ||
      row.posts < 0
  )
) {
  throw new Error(
    "Invalid monthly posts snapshot."
  );
}

const { latestProgress, latestSummary } = findLatestDiary();

if (!latestProgress) {
  throw new Error(
    "No ranged current-progress diary file was found."
  );
}

const progressPath = path.join(
  diaryDir,
  latestProgress
);

const progressMarkdown = readText(progressPath);

const summaryMarkdown = latestSummary
  ? readText(path.join(diaryDir, latestSummary))
  : "";

const combinedText =
  `${progressMarkdown}\n${summaryMarkdown}`;

const rangeMatch = latestProgress.match(
  /^(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})-to-(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/
);

const startDate = rangeMatch?.[1] ?? null;
const endDate = rangeMatch?.[2] ?? null;

const tests = findLastNumber(
  combinedText,
  [
    /(\d+)\s*\/\s*\d+\s*(?:tests?|test)/i,
    /(\d+)\s+(?:automated\s+)?tests?\s+(?:passed|pass)/i,
  ],
  79
);

const analyticsUsers = findLastNumber(
  combinedText,
  [
    /([\d,]+)\s+synthetic\s+users/i,
    /([\d,]+)\s+analytics\s+users/i,
    /dataset\s+(?:of|with|gồm)\s+([\d,]+)\s+users/i,
    /([\d,]+)\s+users/i,
  ],
  600
);

const analyticsPosts = findLastNumber(
  combinedText,
  [
    /([\d,]+)\s+synthetic\s+posts/i,
    /([\d,]+)\s+analytics\s+posts/i,
    /dataset\s+(?:of|with|gồm)\s+([\d,]+)\s+posts/i,
    /([\d,]+)\s+posts/i,
  ],
  2250
);

const postingRate = findLastNumber(
  latestProgress,
  [/Posting rate:\s*([\d.]+)\s*%/i],
  80
);

const publishedPosts = findLastNumber(
  latestProgress,
  [/([\d,]+)\s+published\b/i],
  1575
);

const draftPosts = findLastNumber(
  latestProgress,
  [/([\d,]+)\s+draft\b/i],
  450
);

const archivedPosts = findLastNumber(
  latestProgress,
  [/([\d,]+)\s+archived\b/i],
  225
);

const statusTotal =
  publishedPosts + draftPosts + archivedPosts;

if (statusTotal !== analyticsPosts) {
  throw new Error(
    `Post status total (${statusTotal}) does not match ` +
    `analyticsPosts (${analyticsPosts}).`
  );
}

const statusShares = {
  published: Number(
    ((publishedPosts / statusTotal) * 100).toFixed(2)
  ),
  draft: Number(
    ((draftPosts / statusTotal) * 100).toFixed(2)
  ),
  archived: Number(
    ((archivedPosts / statusTotal) * 100).toFixed(2)
  ),
};

const monthlyPostsTotal = monthlyPosts.reduce(
  (sum, row) => sum + row.posts,
  0
);

if (monthlyPostsTotal !== analyticsPosts) {
  throw new Error(
    `Monthly posts snapshot total (${monthlyPostsTotal}) ` +
    `does not match analyticsPosts (${analyticsPosts}).`
  );
}

const highlights = buildHighlights(
  combinedText,
  {
    tests,
    analyticsUsers,
    analyticsPosts,
  }
);

const analyticsStages = buildAnalyticsRoadmap();

const completedAnalyticsStages =
  analyticsStages.filter(
    (stage) => stage.completed
  ).length;

let currentFocus = "SQL Analytics";

if (
  completedAnalyticsStages ===
  analyticsStages.length
) {
  currentFocus = "Pandas & EDA";
}

const analyticsKpi = JSON.parse(readText(analyticsKpiFile));

if (
  !Number.isInteger(analyticsKpi.totalUsers) ||
  !Number.isInteger(analyticsKpi.allPosts) ||
  !Number.isInteger(analyticsKpi.top10PercentPosts) ||
  analyticsKpi.totalUsers <= 0 ||
  analyticsKpi.allPosts <= 0 ||
  analyticsKpi.top10PercentPosts < 0 ||
  analyticsKpi.top10PercentPosts > analyticsKpi.allPosts
) {
  throw new Error("Invalid analytics KPI snapshot.");
}

if (analyticsKpi.totalUsers !== analyticsUsers) {
  throw new Error(
    `KPI snapshot has ${analyticsKpi.totalUsers} users, ` +
    `but progress data has ${analyticsUsers}. Refresh the KPI snapshot.`
  );
}

if (analyticsKpi.allPosts !== analyticsPosts) {
  throw new Error(
    `KPI snapshot has ${analyticsKpi.allPosts} posts, ` +
    `but progress data has ${analyticsPosts}. Refresh the KPI snapshot.`
  );
}

const topCreatorShare = Number(
  ((analyticsKpi.top10PercentPosts / analyticsKpi.allPosts) * 100).toFixed(2)
);

const data = {
  generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),

  latest: {
    week: extractWeek(combinedText),

    startDate,

    endDate,

    period:
      startDate && endDate
        ? `${formatDate(startDate)} — ${formatDate(endDate)}`
        : "Latest progress",

    progressFile: latestProgress,

    summaryFile: latestSummary,

    highlights,
  },

  stats: {
    tests,
    analyticsUsers,
    analyticsPosts,

    postingRate,

    topCreatorShare,

    postStatuses: {
      published: publishedPosts,
      draft: draftPosts,
      archived: archivedPosts,
    },

    statusShares,
  },

  currentFocus,

  monthlyPosts,

  analyticsStages,
};

// Ensure the output directory exists before writing the generated file.
fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(outputFile), {
  recursive: true,
});

fs.writeFileSync(
  outputFile,
  `${JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)}\n`
);

console.log("✓ Mentor progress data synchronized");
console.log(`  Diary: ${latestProgress}`);
console.log(
  `  Analytics stages: ${completedAnalyticsStages}/${analyticsStages.length}`
);
console.log(`  Current focus: ${currentFocus}`);
```

```tsx
    themeDark: "Giao diện tối",
    language: "Ngôn ngữ",
    periodLabel: "Khoảng thời gian",
    noData: "Không có dữ liệu theo tháng.",
  },
};

const statusColors = ["#16a34a", "#f59e0b", "#64748b"];

export default function Home() {
  const [language, setLanguage] = useState<Language>("en");
  const [theme, setTheme] = useState<Theme>("light");
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState<Period>("all");

  const t = translations[language];
  const isDark = theme === "dark";

  const filteredMonthlyPosts = useMemo(() => {
    if (period === "all") return monthlyPosts;
    return monthlyPosts.slice(-Number(period));
  }, [period]);

  const selectedPeriodTotal = filteredMonthlyPosts.reduce(
    (sum, row) => sum + Number(row.posts || 0),
    0
  );

  const formatNumber = (value: number) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat(language === "en" ? "en-US" : "vi-VN").format(value);

  const formatPercent = (
    value: number,
    maximumFractionDigits = 1
  ) =>
    new Intl.NumberFormat(language === "en" ? "en-US" : "vi-VN", {
      maximumFractionDigits,
    }).format(value) + "%";

  const totalUsers = progress.stats.analyticsUsers;
  const participationRate = progress.stats.postingRate;
  const usersWhoPosted = Math.round(
    (totalUsers * participationRate) / 100
  );

  const statusData = [
    {
      name: t.published,
      value: progress.stats.statusShares.published,
    },
    {
      name: t.draft,
      value: progress.stats.statusShares.draft,
    },
    {
      name: t.archived,
      value: progress.stats.statusShares.archived,
    },
  ];


  const monthlyData = progress.monthlyPosts;
  const latestMonth = monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1];

  // Tháng cuối cùng là tháng chưa hoàn tất nên không dùng để tìm đỉnh.
  const completedMonthlyData = latestMonth
    ? monthlyData.filter((row) => row.month < latestMonth.month)
    : monthlyData;

  const peakMonth = completedMonthlyData.length
    ? completedMonthlyData.reduce((best, row) =>
        row.posts > best.posts ? row : best
      )
    : undefined;

  const formatMonthLabel = (month: string) =>
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat(
      language === "en" ? "en-US" : "vi-VN",
      {
        month: "long",
        year: "numeric",
        timeZone: "UTC",
      }
    ).format(new Date(`${month}-01T00:00:00Z`));

  const datasetScope =
    language === "en"
      ? `${formatNumber(totalUsers)} users · ${formatNumber(progress.stats.analyticsPosts)} posts`
      : `${formatNumber(totalUsers)} người dùng · ${formatNumber(progress.stats.analyticsPosts)} bài đăng`;

  const coverageRange = monthlyData.length
    ? language === "en"
      ? `${formatMonthLabel(monthlyData[0].month)}–${formatMonthLabel(monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1].month)}`
      : `Tháng ${Number(monthlyData[0].month.slice(5, 7))}/${monthlyData[0].month.slice(0, 4)}–tháng ${Number(monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1].month.slice(5, 7))}/${monthlyData[monthlyData.length - 1].month.slice(0, 4)}`
    : "—";

  const primaryText = isDark ? "text-slate-100" : "text-slate-900";
  const secondaryText = isDark ? "text-slate-400" : "text-slate-500";
  const pageBackground = isDark
    ? "bg-[#080d19] text-slate-100"
    : "bg-slate-50 text-slate-900";

  const navItems = [
    { label: t.overview, href: "#overview", icon: LayoutDashboard },
    { label: t.storytelling, href: "#storytelling", icon: TrendingUp },
    { label: t.questions, href: "#questions", icon: Target },
    { label: t.quality, href: "#quality", icon: ShieldCheck },
  ];

  const kpis = [
    {
      label: t.users,
      value: formatNumber(totalUsers),
      note: t.ofUsers,
      icon: Users,
      accent: "text-blue-500",
      iconBg: isDark ? "bg-blue-500/10" : "bg-blue-50",
    },
    {
      label: t.activeUsers,
      value: formatNumber(usersWhoPosted),
      note: t.havePosted,
      icon: Activity,
      accent: "text-emerald-500",
      iconBg: isDark ? "bg-emerald-500/10" : "bg-emerald-50",
    },
    {
      label: t.participation,
      value: formatPercent(participationRate),
      note: t.engagement,
      icon: Target,
      accent: "text-violet-500",
      iconBg: isDark ? "bg-violet-500/10" : "bg-violet-50",
    },
    {
      label: t.totalPosts,
      value: formatNumber(selectedPeriodTotal),
      note: t.periodPosts,
      icon: FileText,
      accent: "text-amber-500",
      iconBg: isDark ? "bg-amber-500/10" : "bg-amber-50",
    },
  ];

  const findings = [
    {
      number: "01",
      title: t.finding1Title,
      description:
        language === "en"
          ? `${formatNumber(usersWhoPosted)} of ${formatNumber(totalUsers)} users have created at least one post, giving the dataset a ${formatPercent(participationRate)} posting-user rate.`
          : `${formatNumber(usersWhoPosted)} trong số ${formatNumber(totalUsers)} người dùng đã tạo ít nhất một bài đăng, tương đương tỷ lệ ${formatPercent(participationRate)}.`,
      meaning: t.finding1Meaning,
      limitation: t.finding1Limit,
      icon: Users,
      color: "text-blue-500",
    },
    {
      number: "02",
      title: t.finding2Title,
      description:
        language === "en"
          ? `The top 10% of creators account for ${formatPercent(progress.stats.topCreatorShare, 2)} of all posts in the dataset.`
          : `Nhóm 10% người tạo nội dung nhiều nhất đóng góp ${formatPercent(progress.stats.topCreatorShare, 2)} tổng số bài đăng.`,
      meaning: t.finding2Meaning,
      limitation: t.finding2Limit,
      icon: Target,
      color: "text-violet-500",
    },
    {
      number: "03",
      title: t.finding3Title,
      description: peakMonth
        ? language === "en"
          ? `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} recorded ${formatNumber(peakMonth.posts)} posts, the highest monthly count among the completed months shown.`
          : `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} ghi nhận ${formatNumber(peakMonth.posts)} bài đăng, cao nhất trong các tháng đã hoàn tất.`
        : language === "en"
          ? "No completed monthly data is available yet."
          : "Chưa có dữ liệu tháng đã hoàn tất.",
      meaning: peakMonth
        ? language === "en"
          ? `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} is a useful period for further investigation of content activity.`
          : `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} là giai đoạn phù hợp để tìm hiểu sâu hơn về hoạt động nội dung.`
        : language === "en"
          ? "Review the monthly data after a complete month becomes available."
          : "Hãy xem xét dữ liệu khi có tháng đã hoàn tất.",
      limitation: latestMonth
        ? language === "en"
          ? `${formatMonthLabel(latestMonth.month)} may be incomplete and should not be compared as a full month.`
          : `${formatMonthLabel(latestMonth.month)} có thể chưa hoàn tất, vì vậy không nên so sánh như một tháng đầy đủ.`
        : language === "en"
          ? "Check the completeness of the monthly data before comparing periods."
          : "Cần kiểm tra độ đầy đủ của dữ liệu tháng trước khi so sánh.",
      icon: TrendingUp,
      color: "text-emerald-500",
    },
  ];

  const businessQuestions = [
    {
      number: "01",
      q: t.q1,
      evidence:
        language === "en"
          ? `${formatNumber(usersWhoPosted)} of ${formatNumber(totalUsers)} users have posted at least once.`
          : `${formatNumber(usersWhoPosted)} trong số ${formatNumber(totalUsers)} người dùng đã đăng ít nhất một bài.`,
      next: t.n1,
    },
    {
      number: "02",
      q: t.q2,
      evidence:
        language === "en"
          ? `The top 10% of creators produce ${formatPercent(progress.stats.topCreatorShare, 2)} of posts.`
          : `Nhóm 10% người tạo nhiều nhất đóng góp ${formatPercent(progress.stats.topCreatorShare, 2)} số bài đăng.`,
      next: t.n2,
    },
    {
      number: "03",
      q: t.q3,
      evidence: peakMonth
        ? language === "en"
          ? `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} has ${formatNumber(peakMonth.posts)} posts, the highest among completed months.`
          : `${formatMonthLabel(peakMonth.month)} có ${formatNumber(peakMonth.posts)} bài đăng, cao nhất trong các tháng đã hoàn tất.`
        : language === "en"
          ? "No completed monthly data is available yet."
          : "Chưa có dữ liệu tháng đã hoàn tất.",
      next: t.n3,
    },
    {
      number: "04",
      q: t.q4,
      evidence:
        language === "en"
          ? `Current status shares are ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.published, 0)} published, ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.draft, 0)} draft, and ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.archived, 0)} archived.`
          : `Tỷ trọng trạng thái hiện tại là ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.published, 0)} đã xuất bản, ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.draft, 0)} bản nháp và ${formatPercent(progress.stats.statusShares.archived, 0)} đã lưu trữ.`,
      next: t.n4,
    },
  ];

  const workflowSteps = [t.step1, t.step2, t.step3, t.step4, t.step5];

  return (
    <main className={`min-h-screen ${pageBackground} transition-colors duration-200`}>
      <div className="flex min-h-screen">
        <aside
          className={`hidden w-64 shrink-0 border-r px-5 py-7 lg:flex lg:flex-col ${
            isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-950" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
          }`}
        >
          <div className="mb-10 flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 text-white shadow-lg shadow-blue-600/20">
              <BarChart3 size={23} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className={`font-bold tracking-tight ${primaryText}`}>InsightLab</p>
              <p className={`text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>Analytics Studio</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className={`mb-3 px-3 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.18em] ${secondaryText}`}>
            {t.workspace}
          </p>

          <nav className="space-y-1">
            {navItems.map((item, index) => {
              const Icon = item.icon;
              return (
                <a
                  key={item.href}
                  href={item.href}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl px-3 py-3 text-sm transition ${
                    index === 0
                      ? "bg-blue-600 font-semibold text-white shadow-md shadow-blue-600/15"
                      : isDark
                        ? "text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-900 hover:text-slate-100"
                        : "text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon size={18} />
                  <span>{item.label}</span>
                  {index === 0 && <ChevronRight className="ml-auto" size={15} />}
                </a>
              );
            })}
          </nav>

          <div className="mt-auto">
            <div
              className={`rounded-2xl border p-4 ${
                isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50"
              }`}
            >
              <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Database size={17} className="text-blue-500" />
                <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${primaryText}`}>{t.sample}</span>
              </div>
              <p className={`text-xs leading-5 ${secondaryText}`}>
                {datasetScope}
              </p>
              <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-emerald-500">
                <CheckCircle2 size={14} />
                <span>{language === "en" ? "Ready for exploration" : "Sẵn sàng phân tích"}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className={`mt-5 text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>Portfolio v1.0</p>
          </div>
        </aside>

        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <header
            className={`sticky top-0 z-20 border-b backdrop-blur-xl ${
              isDark
                ? "border-slate-800 bg-[#080d19]/90"
                : "border-slate-200 bg-white/90"
            }`}
          >
            <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-[1600px] flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:px-6 xl:px-9">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-blue-600 text-white lg:hidden">
                  <BarChart3 size={21} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className={`text-sm font-semibold ${primaryText}`}>InsightLab</p>
                  <p className={`text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>{t.portfolio}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <label className="sr-only" htmlFor="period-filter">
                  {t.periodLabel}
                </label>
                <select
                  id="period-filter"
                  value={period}
                  onChange={(event) => setPeriod(event.target.value as Period)}
                  className={`rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500 ${
                    isDark
                      ? "border-slate-700 bg-slate-900 text-slate-200"
                      : "border-slate-200 bg-white text-slate-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  <option value="all">{t.allTime}</option>
                  <option value="6">{t.last6}</option>
                  <option value="3">{t.last3}</option>
                </select>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setLanguage((current) => current === "en" ? "vi" : "en")}
                  aria-label={t.language}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition ${
                    isDark
                      ? "border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800"
                      : "border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100"
                  }`}
                >
                  <Globe2 size={16} />
                  {language === "en" ? "EN / VI" : "VI / EN"}
                </button>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setTheme((current) => current === "light" ? "dark" : "light")}
                  aria-label={isDark ? t.themeLight : t.themeDark}
                  className={`flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition ${
                    isDark
                      ? "border-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-800"
                      : "border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-100"
                  }`}
                >
                  {isDark ? <Sun size={16} /> : <Moon size={16} />}
                  <span className="hidden sm:inline">
                    {isDark ? t.themeLight : t.themeDark}
                  </span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </header>

          <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px] space-y-8 px-4 py-7 sm:px-6 sm:py-9 xl:px-9">
            <section id="overview">
              <div className="mb-7 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-end">
                <div>
                  <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-1 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide text-blue-500">
                      {t.portfolio}
                    </span>
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1 text-[11px] ${
                        isDark
                          ? "border-slate-700 text-slate-400"
                          : "border-slate-200 text-slate-500"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {t.sample}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <h1 className={`text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight sm:text-4xl ${primaryText}`}>
                    {t.title}
                  </h1>
                  <p className={`mt-3 max-w-3xl text-sm leading-6 sm:text-base ${secondaryText}`}>
                    {t.subtitle}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div
                  className={`flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 ${
                    isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className={`flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${
                    isDark ? "bg-slate-800" : "bg-slate-100"
                  }`}>
                    <Activity size={18} className="text-blue-500" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className={`text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>{t.lastUpdated}</p>
                    <p className={`text-sm font-semibold ${primaryText}`}>
                      {period === "all" ? t.allTime : period === "6" ? t.last6 : t.last3}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-4">
                {kpis.map((kpi) => {
                  const Icon = kpi.icon;
                  return (
                    <article
                      key={kpi.label}
                      className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 shadow-sm transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md ${
                        isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                        <div>
                          <p className={`text-sm font-medium ${secondaryText}`}>{kpi.label}</p>
                          <p className={`mt-3 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight ${primaryText}`}>
                            {kpi.value}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                        <div className={`flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${kpi.iconBg} ${kpi.accent}`}>
                          <Icon size={21} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <p className={`mt-4 text-xs leading-5 ${secondaryText}`}>{kpi.note}</p>
                    </article>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.7fr)_minmax(320px,0.9fr)]">
              <article
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6 ${
                  isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className={`text-lg font-bold ${primaryText}`}>{t.monthlyTitle}</h2>
                    <p className={`mt-1 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{t.monthlySubtitle}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-500">
                    <BarChart3 size={15} />
                    {t.posts}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="h-[300px] w-full">
                  {filteredMonthlyPosts.length > 0 ? (
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                      <BarChart data={filteredMonthlyPosts} margin={{ top: 8, right: 8, left: -18, bottom: 4 }}>
                        <CartesianGrid
                          strokeDasharray="3 3"
                          vertical={false}
                          stroke={isDark ? "#263244" : "#e2e8f0"}
                        />
                        <XAxis
                          dataKey="month"
                          axisLine={false}
                          tickLine={false}
                          tick={{ fill: isDark ? "#94a3b8" : "#64748b", fontSize: 11 }}
                          dy={8}
                        />
                        <YAxis
                          axisLine={false}
                          tickLine={false}
                          tick={{ fill: isDark ? "#94a3b8" : "#64748b", fontSize: 11 }}
                          tickFormatter={(value) => formatNumber(Number(value))}
                        />
                        <Tooltip
                          cursor={{ fill: isDark ? "#1e293b" : "#f1f5f9" }}
                          contentStyle={{
                            backgroundColor: isDark ? "#0f172a" : "#ffffff",
                            border: `1px solid ${isDark ? "#334155" : "#e2e8f0"}`,
                            borderRadius: 12,
                            color: isDark ? "#f8fafc" : "#0f172a",
                          }}
                          labelStyle={{
                            color: isDark ? "#f8fafc" : "#0f172a",
                            fontWeight: 600,
                            marginBottom: 4,
                          }}
                          formatter={(value) => [formatNumber(Number(value)), t.posts]}
                        />
                        <Bar
                          dataKey="posts"
                          name={t.posts}
                          fill="#3b82f6"
                          radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
                          maxBarSize={44}
                        />
                      </BarChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                  ) : (
                    <div className={`flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>
                      {t.noData}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div
                  className={`mt-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t pt-4 ${
                    isDark ? "border-slate-800" : "border-slate-100"
                  }`}
                >
                  <p className={`text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>{t.selectedTotal}</p>
                  <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${primaryText}`}>
                    {formatNumber(selectedPeriodTotal)} {t.posts.toLowerCase()}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </article>

              <article
                className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 shadow-sm sm:p-6 ${
                  isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                }`}
              >
                <div className="mb-3">
                  <h2 className={`text-lg font-bold ${primaryText}`}>{t.statusTitle}</h2>
                  <p className={`mt-1 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{t.statusSubtitle}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="relative h-[230px]">
                  <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                    <PieChart>
                      <Pie
                        data={statusData}
                        dataKey="value"
                        nameKey="name"
                        cx="50%"
                        cy="50%"
                        innerRadius={62}
                        outerRadius={88}
                        paddingAngle={4}
                        stroke="none"
                      >
                        {statusData.map((entry, index) => (
                          <Cell key={entry.name} fill={statusColors[index]} />
                        ))}
                      </Pie>
                      <Tooltip
                        formatter={(value) => [formatPercent(Number(value)), language === "en" ? "Share" : "Tỷ trọng"]}
                        contentStyle={{
                          backgroundColor: isDark ? "#0f172a" : "#ffffff",
                          border: `1px solid ${isDark ? "#334155" : "#e2e8f0"}`,
                          borderRadius: 12,
                          color: isDark ? "#f8fafc" : "#0f172a",
                        }}
                      />
                    </PieChart>
                  </ResponsiveContainer>
                  <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center">
                    <span className={`text-3xl font-bold ${primaryText}`}>100%</span>
                    <span className={`mt-1 text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>
                      {language === "en" ? "All statuses" : "Tất cả trạng thái"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {statusData.map((item, index) => (
                    <div key={item.name} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                        <span
                          className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: statusColors[index] }}
                        />
                        <span className={`text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{item.name}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className={`text-sm font-semibold ${primaryText}`}>
                        {formatPercent(item.value)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </article>
            </section>

            <section id="storytelling">
              <div className="mb-5">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-500">
                  <TrendingUp size={18} />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.16em]">
                    {t.storytelling.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${primaryText}`}>
                  {t.storytellingTitle}
                </h2>
                <p className={`mt-2 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{t.storytellingSubtitle}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {findings.map((finding) => {
                  const Icon = finding.icon;
                  return (
                    <article
                      key={finding.number}
                      className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 sm:p-6 ${
                        isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className={`text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.16em] ${secondaryText}`}>
                          FINDING {finding.number}
                        </span>
                        <div className={`flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${
                          isDark ? "bg-slate-800" : "bg-slate-100"
                        } ${finding.color}`}>
                          <Icon size={20} />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <h3 className={`text-base font-bold leading-6 ${primaryText}`}>
                        {finding.title}
                      </h3>
                      <p className={`mt-3 text-sm leading-6 ${secondaryText}`}>
                        {finding.description}
                      </p>

                      <div className={`mt-5 rounded-xl p-3.5 ${
                        isDark ? "bg-slate-800/70" : "bg-slate-50"
                      }`}>
                        <p className={`text-xs font-semibold ${primaryText}`}>
                          {language === "en" ? "Interpretation" : "Diễn giải"}
                        </p>
                        <p className={`mt-1.5 text-xs leading-5 ${secondaryText}`}>
                          {finding.meaning}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-2">
                        <CircleHelp className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-amber-500" size={15} />
                        <p className={`text-xs leading-5 ${secondaryText}`}>
                          {finding.limitation}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </article>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section id="questions">
              <div className="mb-5">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-violet-500">
                  <Target size={18} />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.16em]">
                    {t.questions.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${primaryText}`}>
                  {t.questionsTitle}
                </h2>
                <p className={`mt-2 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{t.questionsSubtitle}</p>
              </div>

              <div className={`overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border ${
                isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
              }`}>
                <div className={`hidden grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-5 border-b px-6 py-4 md:grid ${
                  isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-950/60" : "border-slate-100 bg-slate-50"
                }`}>
                  <p className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide ${secondaryText}`}>{t.question}</p>
                  <p className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide ${secondaryText}`}>{t.evidence}</p>
                  <p className={`text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide ${secondaryText}`}>{t.nextStep}</p>
                </div>

                {businessQuestions.map((item) => (
                  <div
                    key={item.number}
                    className={`grid gap-3 border-b p-5 last:border-b-0 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)] md:gap-5 md:px-6 ${
                      isDark ? "border-slate-800" : "border-slate-100"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                      <span className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-blue-500/10 text-xs font-bold text-blue-500">
                        {item.number}
                      </span>
                      <div>
                        <p className={`mb-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide md:hidden ${secondaryText}`}>
                          {t.question}
                        </p>
                        <p className={`text-sm font-semibold leading-6 ${primaryText}`}>{item.q}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <p className={`mb-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide md:hidden ${secondaryText}`}>
                        {t.evidence}
                      </p>
                      <p className={`text-sm leading-6 ${secondaryText}`}>{item.evidence}</p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <p className={`mb-1 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide md:hidden ${secondaryText}`}>
                        {t.nextStep}
                      </p>
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                        <ArrowUpRight className="mt-0.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" size={16} />
                        <p className={`text-sm leading-6 ${secondaryText}`}>{item.next}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>

            <section id="quality">
              <div className="mb-5">
                <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-500">
                  <ShieldCheck size={18} />
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.16em]">
                    {t.quality.toUpperCase()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <h2 className={`text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${primaryText}`}>
                  {t.qualityTitle}
                </h2>
                <p className={`mt-2 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>{t.qualitySubtitle}</p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.15fr)_minmax(0,0.85fr)]">
                <article className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 sm:p-6 ${
                  isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                }`}>
                  <div className="space-y-5">
                    {[
                      { label: t.source, value: t.sourceValue, icon: Database },
                      { label: t.scope, value: datasetScope, icon: Users },
                      { label: t.completeness, value: coverageRange, icon: Activity },
                    ].map((item) => {
                      const Icon = item.icon;
                      return (
                        <div key={item.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                          <div className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${
                            isDark ? "bg-slate-800" : "bg-slate-100"
                          }`}>
                            <Icon size={18} className="text-blue-500" />
                          </div>
                          <div>
                            <p className={`text-xs ${secondaryText}`}>{item.label}</p>
                            <p className={`mt-1 text-sm font-semibold leading-6 ${primaryText}`}>
                              {item.value}
                            </p>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>

                  <div className={`mt-6 rounded-xl border p-4 ${
                    isDark
                      ? "border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/5"
                      : "border-amber-200 bg-amber-50/70"
                  }`}>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-500">
                      <CircleHelp size={17} />
                      <p className="text-sm font-bold">{t.caveat}</p>
                    </div>
                    <p className={`mt-2 text-sm leading-6 ${secondaryText}`}>{t.caveatValue}</p>
                  </div>

                  <div className={`mt-3 rounded-xl border p-4 ${
                    isDark
                      ? "border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/50"
                      : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50"
                  }`}>
                    <p className={`text-sm font-bold ${primaryText}`}>{t.limitation}</p>
                    <p className={`mt-2 text-sm leading-6 ${secondaryText}`}>{t.limitationValue}</p>
                  </div>
                </article>

                <article className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 sm:p-6 ${
                  isDark ? "border-slate-800 bg-slate-900" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"
                }`}>
                  <h3 className={`text-lg font-bold ${primaryText}`}>{t.workflow}</h3>
                  <p className={`mt-1 text-sm ${secondaryText}`}>
                    {language === "en"
                      ? "A repeatable process for an internship or fresher project."
                      : "Một quy trình có thể áp dụng cho dự án thực tập hoặc Fresher."}
                  </p>

                  <div className="mt-6 space-y-0">
                    {workflowSteps.map((step, index) => (
                      <div key={step} className="flex gap-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-col items-center">
                          <div className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                            index === workflowSteps.length - 1
                              ? "bg-emerald-500 text-white"
                              : "bg-blue-600 text-white"
                          }`}>
                            {index === workflowSteps.length - 1 ? (
                              <CheckCircle2 size={18} />
                            ) : (
                              index + 1
                            )}
                          </div>
                          {index < workflowSteps.length - 1 && (
                            <div className={`my-1 min-h-7 w-px flex-1 ${
                              isDark ? "bg-slate-700" : "bg-slate-200"
                            }`} />
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="pb-6 pt-1">
                          <p className={`text-sm font-medium leading-6 ${primaryText}`}>{step}</p>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </article>
              </div>
            </section>

            <footer className={`border-t pt-5 ${
              isDark ? "border-slate-800" : "border-slate-200"
            }`}>
              <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-2 pb-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
                <p className={`text-xs leading-5 ${secondaryText}`}>{t.footer}</p>
                <a
                  href="#overview"
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-semibold text-blue-500 hover:text-blue-400"
                >
                  {t.overview}
                  <ArrowDownRight size={14} />
                </a>
              </div>
            </footer>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}
```
